#### [2. Awesome Webgl](/content/sjfricke/awesome-webgl/README.md)

##### Bug Reporting

* [Chrome Bug Report](https://issues.chromium.org/issues) - Chrome related bugs
* [WebKit Bugzilla](https://bugs.webkit.org/enter_bug.cgi?product=WebKit\&component=WebGL) - Safari related bugs

##### GLSL Editors

* [GLSL Sandbox](https://glslsandbox.com) - Online live editor for fragment shaders.
* [ShaderExpo](https://anuraghazra.dev/ShaderExpo/) - Dependency free shader editor featuring inline error logs, auto completions, models and textures loading.
* [WebGL Playground](http://jessevdk.github.io/webgl-play/) - The editor lets you work on the JavaScript code and the GLSL vertex/fragment shaders (if you have any) at the same time in a convenient way. Everything is organized, formatted and highlighted properly, just as you would like.

##### References

* [Khronos Official Wiki](https://wikis.khronos.org/webgl/) - The official wiki for WebGL.
* [WebGL Errata](https://wikis.khronos.org/webgl/Errata_to_the_WebGL_Specification) - Known bugs in graphics drivers affect the conformance suite, and consequently, portability of code.
* [WebGL Extensions](https://registry.khronos.org/webgl/extensions/) - List of extensions for WebGL
* [WebGL Spec Sheet](https://registry.khronos.org/webgl/specs/1.0/) - All the detailed information about WebGL.

##### Talks

* [List of Presentations](https://wikis.khronos.org/webgl/Presentations) - List presented by Khronos of various WebGL related presentations.

##### Tools/Debugging

* [WebGL Report](https://webglreport.com/?v=1) - Way to view the details of what your browser supports for WebGL.
* [WebGL Support Stats](https://webglstats.com/) - Interactive dashboard showing the support for WebGL features in different browsers and devices.

##### Tools/Debugging / Chrome Specific Tools/Debugger

* [Spector.js Extension](https://chromewebstore.google.com/detail/spectorjs/denbgaamihkadbghdceggmchnflmhpmk) - Explore and Troubleshoot your WebGL and WebGL2 scenes easily.

##### Tools/Debugging / Firefox Specific Tools/Debugger

* [Firefox Developer Tools](https://firefox-source-docs.mozilla.org/devtools-user/index.html) - The official list of all of Firefox's debugger tools.

##### Tutorials / Firefox Specific Tools/Debugger

* [Directional Shadow Mapping](https://chinedufn.com/webgl-shadow-mapping-tutorial/) - Concepts behind real time directional light shadow mapping.
* [Get Started Tutorial](https://wikis.khronos.org/webgl/Tutorial) - Khronos' tutorial how to get up and running with WebGL.
* [Multitexturing using a Blendmap](https://chinedufn.com/webgl-multitexture-blend-map-tutorial/) - How to use a blendmap to multitexture a terrain.
* [Particle Effects via Billboards](https://chinedufn.com/webgl-particle-effect-billboard-tutorial/) - Create particle effects by applying a technique called billboarding.
* [WebGL Workshop](https://webgl-workshop.com/) - Interactive workshop to get you up and running with WebGL.

##### References / Firefox Specific Tools/Debugger

* [WebGL 2 Spec Sheet (Editor Draft)](https://registry.khronos.org/webgl/specs/latest/2.0/) - All the detailed information about WebGL 2.
* [Immersive Web Community Group](https://www.w3.org/community/immersive-web/) - Group whose goal is to help bring high-performance Virtual Reality and Augmented Reality to the open Web.

##### Engines and Frameworks / Firefox Specific Tools/Debugger

#### [3. Free for Dev](/content/ripienaar/free-for-dev/README.md)

##### CI and CD

* [Stategraph](https://stategraph.com) - GitOps-first Terraform automation with pull request-driven workflows, project isolation via self-hosted runners, and layered runs for ordered operations. Free for up to 3 users.

##### Monitoring

* [Pingzen](https://pingzen.dev) - Free uptime and API monitoring with up to 55 monitors, 1-minute check intervals, and instant alerts.

##### DNS

* [nip.io](https://nip.io/) - Free DNS service that when queried with a hostname with an embedded IP address returns that IP address.

##### IaaS

* [Out Plane](https://outplane.com) - Cloud sandboxes for coding agents (Hangars) plus app hosting in Germany. Free: Hangars, cloud machines where agents like Claude Code, Codex and Cursor run, with no credit card required; the agent uses your own plan or API key. App hosting is paid, from $9/month with a 14-day free trial.

##### Audio & Speech

* [SightRadar Face Recognition API MCP](https://sightradar.com/mcp) - Official MCP server for the SightRadar face recognition API: index photos, find every photo of a person from one selfie, and compare or detect faces from Claude, ChatGPT, Cursor or VS Code. #### [12. Free for Dev](/content/ripienaar/free-for-dev/README.md)

##### APIs, Data, and ML

* [VPNDetection](https://vpndetection.io) - VPN, proxy and hosting detection API for any IP address. The free plan answers the VPN, Tor and relay flags, country and ASN, with no API key, signup or monthly cap.

##### Email

* [Alias Email](https://alias.email) - Free 10 Bump email addresses, one custom domain
* [UserCheck](https://www.usercheck.com) - Prevent users to sign up with temporary email addresses, 120 requests/hour (\~86,400 per month)

##### Forms

* [WaiverCat](https://waivercat.com) - Electronic Signature platform with a REST API. You can receive notifications with webhooks. Free plan watermarks signed documents but allow unlimited envelopes + signatures.

#### [3. Awesome Bigdata](/content/newTendermint/awesome-bigdata/README.md)

##### Distributed Tracing Platform

* [Zipkin](https://zipkin.io/) - Zipkin is a distributed tracing system. It helps gather timing data needed to troubleshoot latency problems in service architectures. Features include both the collection and lookup of this data.

##### SQL-like processing

* [Timeplus Proton (⭐2.3k)](https://github.com/timeplus-io/proton) ⭐ 2,285 | 🐛 75 | 🌐 C++ | 📅 2026-11-10 - streaming SQL engine in a single C++ binary, powered by ClickHouse, for streaming ETL, windowed aggregations, joins and materialized views over Kafka and other sources.
* [Opteryx](https://opteryx.app/) - hosted single-node SQL engine that queries Parquet and JSONL files where they live (GCS, S3, HTTP), with Iceberg and PostgreSQL sources.

##### Interesting Readings

* [WebSocket fan-out with Redis pub/sub](https://sifting.io/blog/websocket-fan-out-redis-pub-sub-one-market-data-connection) - Practical guide to sharing one upstream data stream across services, with subscription budgets, last-value caching and reconnect handling.

#### [4. Awesome Billing](/content/kdeldycke/awesome-billing/README.md)

##### Basics

* [Billing systems are a nightmare for engineers](https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=31424450) - There is a shadow army of billing software engineers behind the biggest companies of the world. Here they share their nightmare stories.
* How to recruit software engineers in this domain? “The trick is to make the accounting/billing/payment department the antechamber of data engineering.” — me, 2021-08-03

##### Pricing / Usage-based Pricing

* “Billing is not complex on purpose: it's the price to pay for elasticity.” — me, [2020-01-06](https://web.archive.org/web/20200110052254/https://twitter.com/kdeldycke/status/1214160678363246592). Or why you're likely to get an endless stream of complaining users if choosing utility pricing scheme: while accurate to the (milli-)cent, this model is frustrating for customers not ready to invest time grasping the underlying concepts.
* [Billing pipeline: a critical time sensitive system](https://kevin.deldycke.com/2020/billing-pipeline-critical-time-sensitive-system) - Design notes on Scaleway's billing pipeline, built to “track nano-euros at the milli-seconds time granularity”. My takeaways: get the current time from one central method, randomize the order of the tests, and do not deploy in the first days of the month, when the accounting reports are produced.

##### Calculator / Market Research

* [Cloudorado](https://web.archive.org/web/20250917102424/https://www.cloudorado.com/) - 💸 A comparison matrix which use ECU (Amazon's vCPU) as a CPU power measure unit. Operated by Cloudorado as a commercial cloud-comparison product.

##### Payments / Electronic invoices

* [Whop](https://go.nli.one/billing) - Platform to accept payments, manage subscriptions and sell software, services or digital products worldwide, at competitive fees. Partner link: the sponsor of this list earns a commission from Whop, at no extra cost to you.
* How ACH works: A developer perspective, [part 1](https://engineering.gusto.com/how-ach-works-a-developer-perspective-part-1/), [part 2](https://engineering.gusto.com/how-ach-works-a-developer-perspective-part-2/), [part 3](https://engineering.gusto.com/how-ach-works-a-developer-perspective-part-3/), [part 4](https://engineering.gusto.com/how-ach-works-a-developer-perspective-part-4/), [part 5](https://engineering.gusto.com/how-ach-works-a-developer-perspective-part-5/).

##### Payments / Online Payments

* [UPI 101: The Basics](https://web.archive.org/web/20250521190704/https://blog.setu.co/articles/upi-101-the-basics) - “In this article, we will learn about India's Unified Payments Interface. A four-year-old payment scheme that has been accounting for 40-45% of digital payments across India.”
* [The untold story of Stripe](https://web.archive.org/web/20230716194531/https://www.wired.co.uk/article/stripe-payments-apple-amazon-facebook) - In which we learn that “once turnover hit a certain level, Paypal automatically put the business on a 21 to 60 day rolling reserve, meaning that up to 30 per cent of a company's revenue could be locked up for up to two months.”

##### Fraud / Statistics

* [JPMorgan Chase v. Javice](https://storage.courtlistener.com/recap/gov.uscourts.ded.81082/gov.uscourts.ded.81082.1.0.pdf#page=41) - Like Benford's law, applied to Excel: “JPMC first noticed irregularities with the list when a JPMC employee observed that the list contained exactly 1,048,576 rows, the maximum permitted by Microsoft Excel.” A count that stops at the limit of a tool is a sign of truncated or made-up data. Here, the executives of a startup sold for $175 million had bought that list of customers from a marketing firm.

##### Business Intelligence / Metrics

* [Thinking about growth and profit](https://web.archive.org/web/20200711083123/https://jlongster.com/thinking-growth-profit) - Discuss the relationship between investments, profit and growth, and how it influence decisions on pricing, free trials and plan structure.

##### History / Cloud providers

* [Payroll consolidation, circa 1965](https://web.archive.org/web/20240917025608/https://collections.museumsvictoria.com.au/items/383697) - A program from the punched card era: a plugboard buried under a tangle of patch wires.

##### Humour / Cloud providers

* “Every time someone violates the arrow of time, the billing department throw a kitty in a black hole.” — me, 2016-09-01

#### [12. Awesome Gemini Cli](/content/Piebald-AI/awesome-gemini-cli/README.md)

##### Commands & Extensions

* [Wavegrain (⭐1)](https://github.com/wavegrain/wavegrain-mcp) ⭐ 1 | 🐛 0 | 📅 2026-11-13 - Curated slideshows and carousels for your brand on autopilot, from Gemini CLI: plan posts for a brand, make TikTok slideshows and Instagram carousels, change slide text, put posts on the Wavegrain Calendar and export them; Wavegrain schedules and posts them for you. Remote MCP server with OAuth sign-in; requires a Wavegrain account. Remote endpoint: `https://wavegrain.com/api/mcp`. Install: `gemini extensions install https://github.com/wavegrain/wavegrain-mcp`, then `/mcp auth wavegrain`.

##### MCP Servers

* [ScanMalware (⭐2)](https://github.com/scanmalware/scanmalware-plugin) ⭐ 2 | 🐛 0 | 📅 2026-11-12 - Keyless remote MCP server and Gemini CLI extension for investigating phishing and malicious URLs. Gemini CLI can submit a link to be run in isolated analysis environments and checked against YARA, antivirus and IDS rules, threat feeds and AI (suspicious pages get an AI verdict with a scam type such as credential phishing), then pivot across the public scan archive by domain, IP, ASN, JARM, favicon hash or script hash. A bundled skill walks from one phishing URL to the rest of its campaign. Install: `gemini extensions install https://github.com/scanmalware/scanmalware-plugin`
